Specifically, Equirectangular Projection (ERP) suffers from severe polar stretching distortions, while cube map projection introduces discontinuities across cube-face boundaries, resulting in degraded feature discriminability and compromised geometric consistency. To address these limitations, we propose TDFNet, the first Tri-projection Deformable Fusion Network for panoramic salient object detection, exploiting complementary projection representations to alleviate geometric distortions and improve detection performance.Specifically, we design a cross-projection deformable attention (CDA) module that leverages spatial correspondences between different projections to construct geometry-aware sampling locations, guiding deformable attention for cross-projection contextual aggregation and enhancing robustness against projection-induced deformations. Furthermore, we introduce a latitude-guided fusion module, which utilizes spherical latitude priors to construct geometric confidence weights for adaptively balancing ERP and CMP features. Meanwhile, LGF incorporates distortion-reduced semantic references from Tangent Projection to achieve cross-projection feature refinement and spatial alignment.By constructing a three-branch encoding architecture based on ERP, CMP, and Tangent Projection, TDFNet simultaneously preserves global spatial continuity, local geometric details, and fine-grained boundary information.

</details>


### [65] [Label-Free Foundational Model Selection for Medical Image Classification under Distribution Shift via Pseudo Label Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25810)
*Juan Iñaki Larrea,Lucas Mansilla,Enzo Ferrante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标签的模型选择方法SUDO，通过测量预测概率分区内伪标签不一致性来评估医学图像分析中基础模型在目标域的表现，使用AURCC评分在无目标域标注情况下有效排名多个视觉-语言模型（如BioMedCLIP、CXR-CLIP等），在跨医院数据分布偏移场景下表现优异，其排名与真实排名相关性高达Spearman rho 0.943（p<0.05），尤其在源数据量少时优于传统基于源域准确率的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际医疗影像部署中，由于不同医疗机构间的数据分布差异，基础模型性能不稳定且缺乏目标域标签难以评估，因此亟需一种无需目标标签即可选择最佳模型的方法，特别是在资源受限环境下。

Method: 提出SUDO框架，将未标注的目标数据按预测概率分块，计算每块内的伪标签不一致性，聚合后得到无需目标标注或微调的AURCC评分，用于对候选模型进行排序。

Result: AURCC评分在多种跨医院数据分布偏移场景下能准确反映模型性能，与真实排名高度一致（Spearman rho最高达0.943），在源数据量小的情况下显著优于仅依赖源域准确率的基线方法。

Conclusion: SUDO结合AURCC评分提供了一种有效的无标签模型选择机制，适用于资源受限且缺乏目标域标注的临床AI部署场景，具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed for medical image analysis. However, under the inter-institutional distribution shift typical of deployment, their performance varies widely and cannot be known without target-domain labels, which are rarely available. This leaves a practical question unresolved: given several candidate foundational models and labeled-data from a source domain, which one to deploy in an unlabeled target domain? We propose a label-free selection criterion built on SUDO, a framework for evaluating clinical AI systems without ground-truth annotations. SUDO partitions the unlabeled target data by predicted probability and, for each region, measures a pseudo-label discrepancy reflecting class contamination; aggregated across regions, this yields a score (AURCC) requiring neither target annotation nor fine-tuning. We show that AURCC can be used to rank a variety of vision-language models (BioMedCLIP, CXR-CLIP, CheXzero, MedCLIP, MedImageInsight, CLIP) on chest X-ray classification across three inter-hospital shift scenarios, under zero-shot and MLP-probe regimes. The AURCC ranking recovers the ground-truth ranking with Spearman rho up to 0.943 (p<0.05). Against the natural baseline of ranking by held-out source accuracy, AURCC is competitive when the labeled source is large and yields a more accurate ranking once it is small; the regime of interest in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [66] [Steer the Sampling, Not the Kernel Grid: Geometry-Guided Sampling Operator for Volumetric Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25819)
*Sizhe Wang,Himashi Peiris,Zhaolin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何引导的局部算子，用于改善3D医学图像分割中细长结构（如血管）的边界精度。该算子通过在每个体素处预测局部方向并进行对称采样，生成紧凑的几何与边界线索，同时在跳接连接处实现跨尺度一致性，减少几何失配。替换3D U-Net中的所有步长为1和2的操作后，在BraTS、MSD Hepatic Vessel和TDSC-ABUS数据集上均取得显著性能提升，尤其在边界指标上表现优异（如BraTS Dice从86.1升至88.9，HD95从7.1降至6.2；TDSC-ABUS HD95从39.1降至27.8），同时参数量从2.3M降至0.8M。该算子还可无缝集成到nnU-Net、Swin-UNETR和MedNeXt等主流架构中，无需修改整体结构即可获得一致性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D U-Net等编码器-解码器网络在处理细长、薄状解剖/病理结构时，因重复下采样和固定网格卷积导致特征模糊或混叠，削弱方向信息，早期错误传播至多尺度，影响分割边界精度，尤其在血管等关键结构上易出现分支断连，改变临床相关拓扑关系。

Method: 提出一种几何引导的局部算子，不改变卷积核形状，而是根据每个体素的局部方向预测有界步长，对称采样并融合成紧凑的几何与边界特征；在跳接路径中引入跨尺度共识机制，对齐编码器与解码器特征，缓解几何失配问题。该算子可统一应用于特征精炼（步长1）与分辨率降低（步长>1）场景。

Result: 在BraTS、MSD Hepatic Vessel和TDSC-ABUS三个基准数据集上，替换原网络中的所有步长1和步长2操作后，分割性能显著提升，特别是边界指标（Dice、HD95）明显改善；模型参数量从2.3M降至0.8M，效率更高。此外，该算子可灵活嵌入多种主流网络架构（如nnU-Net、Swin-UNETR、MedNeXt），无需调整宏观结构即可获得稳定性能增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何引导局部算子有效提升了3D医学图像中细长结构的分割精度，尤其改善了边界保持能力，且具有良好的通用性与轻量化特性，适用于多种主流网络架构，为临床定量分析与解剖映射提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Accurate 3D segmentation is central to quantitative lesion assessment and anatomy mapping for clinical planning and follow-up. Thin, elongated, and fine anatomical/pathological structures (e.g., vessels) are a particularly challenging case: a one-voxel boundary error can disconnect a branch and change clinically relevant topology. In encoder-decoder networks (e.g., U-Net), repeated downsampling and fixed-grid convolution blur or alias fine structures and weaken orientation cues, so early mistakes propagate across scales. We propose a geometry-guided local operator that steers where features are sampled, rather than deforming convolutional kernels, under a single formulation for both feature refinement (stride 1) and resolution reduction (stride > 1). At each voxel, it predicts a local orientation and bounded step sizes, samples symmetrically along these directions, and transforms paired samples into compact geometric and boundary cues with lightweight mixing; a cross-scale consensus aligns encoder and decoder features at skip connections to reduce geometric mismatch. Replacing all stride 1 and stride 2 operators in a 3D U-Net yields consistent improvements on BraTS, MSD Hepatic Vessel, and TDSC-ABUS, with notably better boundary metrics (e.g., BraTS Dice 86.1 to 88.9, HD95 7.1 to 6.2; TDSC-ABUS HD95 39.1 to 27.8) while reducing parameters from 2.3M to 0.8M. We further demonstrate that the operator can be integrated into other backbones (e.g., nnU-Net, Swin-UNETR, and MedNeXt) without changing their macro-architectures while providing consistent performance gains.

</details>


### [67] [FlowMoDL: Model-Based Deep Learning with Conjugate-Gradient Data Consistency for Highly Accelerated 4D Flow MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25828)
*Tristan Gottwald,Michelle Bruch,Mubashir-Ul Hassan,Fatma Alickovic,Milan Kloiber,Daniel Tenbrinck,Torsten Panholzer,Melanie Schaller,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowMoDL 是一种用于加速 4D 流动 MRI 重建的可展开神经网络，通过结合 (3+1)D 时空去噪器与基于 SENSE 前向模型的共轭梯度数据一致性更新，实现对解剖结构和速度场的联合优化。采用双路径条件机制适应不同加速因子（10× 到 50×），并使用深度监督复合损失函数（包含速度幅值与角度误差惩罚）和课程学习策略提升生理准确性。在多中心 CMRx4DFlow 数据集上，相比传统方法及现有深度学习基线（如 CG-SENSE、MoDL、FlowVN、FlowMRI-Net），FlowMoDL 在有限梯度步数下仍具更强鲁棒性与优越性能，在所有加速因子下均显著优于对手，尤其在结构清晰度与速度场时间一致性方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 4D 流动 MRI 重建受限于采集时间，亟需高效高精度算法；现有深度学习方法在高加速下性能下降明显，且难以同时保证解剖结构与速度场的双重准确性。因此需要一种能高效处理高加速、兼具结构与速度保真度的新型重建框架。

Method: 基于 MoDL 框架，提出 FlowMoDL，其核心为交替执行 (3+1)D 时空去噪器（学习部分）与共轭梯度数据一致性更新（物理模型部分），引入双路径条件机制动态调节去噪器特征与数据一致性权重，支持跨加速因子泛化；训练时采用深度监督复合损失，显式惩罚速度幅值与角度误差，并配合课程学习策略稳定训练过程。

Result: 在相同梯度步数预算下，FlowMoDL 显著优于所有对比方法（包括 FlowVN 与 FlowMRI-Net 等专用流体网络），在所有加速因子（10×–50×）下均实现更高的幅度 SSIM、更低的 nRMSE、相对速度误差与角度误差；成功恢复了锐利的解剖细节与时间一致的速度场。

Conclusion: FlowMoDL 通过融合物理模型与深度学习优势，实现了在高加速条件下对 4D 流动 MRI 的高效、高精度重建，具备优异的泛化能力与梯度步效率，是当前最先进且实用的 4D 流动 MRI 重建方法之一。

Abstract: We present FlowMoDL, an unrolled neural network for highly accelerated 4D flow MRI reconstruction that directly optimizes for both anatomical magnitude and phase-derived velocity accuracy. Building on the MoDL framework, FlowMoDL alternates a learned (3+1)D spatiotemporal denoiser with conjugate-gradient data-consistency updates based on the SENSE forward model. A novel dual-pathway conditioning scheme adapts the denoiser features and data-consistency weighting, enabling a single model to handle varying acceleration factors ($10\times$ to $50\times$). To ensure physiological accuracy, the network is trained using a deep-supervision composite loss that explicitly penalizes velocity magnitude and angular errors, stabilized by a curriculum schedule. We evaluate FlowMoDL on the multi-center CMRx4DFlow dataset against classical and deep-learning baselines (CG-SENSE, MoDL, FlowVN, and FlowMRI-Net). A key advantage of FlowMoDL is its superior gradient step efficiency. When evaluated under an equivalent, limited budget of gradient steps, competing flow-specific networks degrade significantly. In contrast, FlowMoDL robustly converges and strictly outperforms all competitors across all acceleration factors in magnitude SSIM, nRMSE, relative velocity error, and angular error, successfully recovering sharp structural details and temporally coherent velocity fields.

</details>


### [68] [Socialized Detector Learning: Trajectory-Guided and Reciprocal Distillation for Heterogeneous Object Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25836)
*Weihao Li,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Ruipu Zhao,Boan Tao,Xinjie Yao,Yan Fan,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TGRD的轨迹引导和相互蒸馏方法，用于异构、类别专业化的目标检测器的社会化学习。该方法通过预计算转移难度得分表并构建最优知识传递路径，实现知识在检测器间的渐进式整合与回传，在不牺牲原有性能的前提下显著提升对新类别的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在知识融合时缺乏对知识传递顺序的规划，或仅支持单向增强，无法有效利用异构检测器间互补的类别支持。本文旨在通过有序的知识迁移机制，实现检测器群体的协同进化。

Method: 提出TGRD方法，基于特征对齐残差估计检测器间转移难度（IDTD），预计算固定得分表，并贪婪地生成知识传递轨迹；沿轨迹将知识逐步整合到统一类别载体中，再通过相互蒸馏返回给各专家检测器。

Result: 在MS COCO数据集上，使用四个异构专家和两种初始化方式，最终载体性能优于同时聚合基线2.6 AP；相互蒸馏后的检测器在未支持类别上达到20.8–28.4 AP，且原性能损失不超过1.3 AP。

Conclusion: 顺序感知的渐进式知识整合结合相互回传，是实现异构目标检测器社会协同演化的有效机制。

Abstract: Object detection knowledge is fragmented across independently trained, heterogeneous detectors with complementary category supports. In socialized learning, this knowledge resides in a society, and learning aims to evolve the society collectively through exchange. However, aggregation-based socialization does not explicitly plan transfer order, whereas progressive multi-teacher distillation considers order but remains a one-way student enhancement in a shared category space. Building on Socialized Learning, we formulate Socialized Detector Learning (SDL) for heterogeneous, category-specialized object detectors and propose Trajectory-Guided and Reciprocal Distillation (TGRD).TGRD estimates directed operational Inter-Detector Transfer Difficulty (IDTD) from held-out feature-alignment residuals, precomputes a fixed score table, and greedily constructs a carrier trajectory. Along the trajectory, knowledge is progressively consolidated into a union-category carrier and then returned to experts through reciprocal transfer. A conditional proxy-certificate analysis shows that, under stated assumptions, the progressive certificate is no larger than an aggregated-target counterpart. On MS COCO with four heterogeneous experts and two carrier initializations, final carriers outperform epoch-matched simultaneous aggregation controls by 2.6 AP in both settings. Reciprocal detectors attain 20.8--28.4 AP on previously unsupported categories while remaining within 1.3 AP of original expert-specific performance. These results support order-aware progressive consolidation followed by reciprocal transfer as a viable mechanism for detector-society evolution.

</details>


### [69] [THA-Flow Generative Model: Prosthesis Geometry Prediction from Preoperative CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25845)
*Yiping Wang,Jie Li,Jingyu Shen,Liao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: THA-Flow 是一种基于条件流匹配的生成模型，能够根据术前CT直接生成三维假体几何形状。该模型通过AutoencoderKL压缩骨结构和假体几何，并利用3D UNet在骨骼条件和可选参数下学习从高斯噪声到假体潜在空间的修正流。在1,355个髋关节的回顾性队列中，模型实现了高保真度的假体生成（PSNR 47.11 dB，SSIM 0.9964），并成功生成了覆盖93.4%患者群体的七种主要股骨柄模型。重复采样保持了组件位置、对线及关键骨-假体界面的一致性，同时允许局部几何变化。这是首个将生成式AI应用于THA三维手术规划的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统THA术前规划通常选择单一假体配置，但同一解剖结构可能对应多个合理方案，因此规划本质上是‘一对多’问题，需用条件概率分布表示。现有方法难以高效生成多样化且符合解剖约束的假体设计，亟需更智能的生成式建模工具支持个性化手术规划。

Method: 提出THA-Flow模型，结合AutoencoderKL压缩术前骨骼与假体几何，采用3D UNet学习在骨骼条件下的流匹配过程，实现从噪声到假体潜空间的映射；通过双通道截断符号距离场表示术后假体，并基于注册数据评估生成质量。

Result: 模型在验证集上达到47.11 dB的峰值信噪比和0.9964的结构相似性指数；成功生成完整的髋臼与股骨假体几何，覆盖93.4%的临床常见股骨柄类型；重复采样保持了关键解剖对齐关系，同时引入合理局部变异。

Conclusion: THA-Flow是首个将生成式AI应用于全髋关节置换术三维规划的研究，展示了其在生成多样化、解剖合理且可重复的假体设计方案方面的潜力，为智能化手术规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Preoperative planning for total hip arthroplasty (THA) is commonly framed as selecting a single prosthesis configuration and placement for a patient's osseous anatomy. In practice, however, the same anatomy may admit several clinically reasonable solutions, making planning inherently a one-to-many problem that is better represented by a conditional probability distribution. We present THA-Flow, a conditional flow-matching model that generates three-dimensional prosthesis geometry directly from preoperative CT. Separate AutoencoderKL models compress preoperative bone anatomy and prosthesis geometry, while a three-dimensional UNet learns a rectified flow from Gaussian noise to the prosthesis latent space under spatial bone conditioning and optional structured prosthesis parameters. The retrospective cohort comprised 1,355 hips from 1,149 patients undergoing primary THA. Following rigid registration of postoperative CT to preoperative CT, the actual postoperative prostheses were transformed independently according to the pelvic and femoral registrations and represented as a dual-channel truncated signed distance field. The prosthesis autoencoder achieved a peak signal-to-noise ratio of 47.11 dB and a structural similarity index of 0.9964 on the validation set. Complete acetabular and femoral geometries were generated across seven major stem models representing 93.4% of the cohort. Repeated bone-conditioned sampling preserved component position, alignment, and the principal bone-prosthesis interfaces while allowing limited local geometric variation. To our knowledge, THA-Flow represents the first application of generative AI to three-dimensional surgical planning for THA.

</details>


### [70] [DEFUSE: Generalizable Backdoor Defense for Self-Supervised Encoders with Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25851)
*Tuo Chen,Jie Gui,Minjing Dong,Lanting Fang,Ju Jia,Benlei Cui,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEFUSE是一种通用的自监督学习编码器后门检测框架，通过将后门检测建模为条件扩散生成模型下的表示条件图像似然估计问题，利用语义重建来识别异常。该方法不依赖于复杂的先验知识，且在视觉和视觉-语言SSL模型上均表现优异，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御方法通常仅针对单一范式（如视觉或视觉-语言SSL），且依赖难以满足的假设（如无污染分布数据或预计算伪标签），因此亟需一种通用、低依赖性的检测框架。

Method: DEFUSE基于贝叶斯后验推断，将后门检测转化为表示条件的图像似然估计问题，采用微调的预训练扩散模型进行语义重建，在参考编码器提供的分离表示空间中评估结果，以区分正常与后门样本。

Result: 实验表明，DEFUSE在多种攻击设置下均显著优于现有检测器，对视觉和视觉-语言SSL编码器具有良好的泛化能力，并大幅降低对受害者编码器或攻击策略先验知识的依赖。

Conclusion: DEFUSE是一种高效、通用且低假设的后门检测框架，适用于多种自监督学习场景，为提升SSL系统的安全性提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) encoders are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, posing threats to both visual SSL encoders and vision-language encoders. Existing defenses are typically designed for only one of these paradigms and rely on restrictive assumptions such as access to uninfected in-distribution data or precomputed pseudo-labels, which are difficult to satisfy in practice. To address these limitations, we propose DEFUSE, a generalizable backdoor detection framework for SSL encoders. Inspired by Bayesian posterior inference, we reformulate backdoor detection as a representation-conditioned image likelihood estimation problem parameterized by a conditional diffusion generative model. Uninfected representations tend to yield semantically consistent reconstructions, whereas backdoored ones are more likely to be mapped to the attacker's target class or semantically meaningless images, deviating from the original semantics and thereby exposing the backdoor. However, we find that the exact likelihood is intractable, because highly abstracted representations discard the low-level information necessary for pixel-faithful reconstruction. We therefore relax the objective to semantic reconstruction and evaluate it in a well-separated representation space provided by a reference encoder. Rather than training from scratch, we fine-tune a pretrained diffusion model, leveraging its generative prior to map data onto the natural image manifold while preserving semantic content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DEFUSE substantially outperforms existing detectors across diverse attack settings, generalizing to both visual SSL and vision-language encoders. Notably, our method greatly reduces the reliance on prior knowledge about the victim encoder or the attack strategy. The source code is available at https://github.com/jsrdcht/DEFUSE .

</details>


### [71] [Precipitation Downscaling Using Foundation Model-Conditioned Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25858)
*Victor Nascimento Ribeiro,Jorge Guevara,Jorge Sebastian Moraga,Chris Lucas,Natalie Lord,Andrew Taylor,Edward Lockhart,Will Trojak,Johannes Schmude,Anne Jones*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了三种条件生成策略在基于扩散模型的每日降水降尺度中的应用：上采样粗分辨率预测因子的通道拼接、使用学习卷积编码器的交叉注意力条件生成，以及使用预训练Prithvi WxC天气基础模型冻结编码器的交叉注意力条件生成。结果表明，交叉注意力条件生成相比简单拼接能更好地保留降水分布的真实性和极端事件特征，尤其在数据有限的情况下，预训练模型条件生成表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率降水场对水文影响评估至关重要，但全球气候模型输出过于粗糙且存在偏差，难以直接使用。现有基于AI的统计降尺度方法虽有潜力，但其大尺度大气预测因子如何调节生成过程仍不明确。因此需要探索更有效的条件生成策略以提升降尺度精度与真实性。

Method: 采用去噪扩散概率模型进行每日降水降尺度，比较三种条件生成策略：通道拼接、学习卷积编码器的交叉注意力，以及使用预训练Prithvi WxC模型冻结编码器的交叉注意力。所有方法在相同条件下进行评估，并使用概率、分布、谱分析和极端事件指标评价性能。

Result: 通道拼接在点位CRPS和MSE上表现最佳，但导致降水场过度平滑，抑制高强度事件；交叉注意力条件生成显著提升分布真实性和谱保真度，尤其在极端事件保留方面优势明显，如Prithvi-WxC条件模型可保留超过一半>100mm/day事件。在全数据集训练下，学习编码器方法与基础模型方法性能相当，但后者计算成本更低。值得注意的是，仅用五年数据训练的Prithvi-WxC条件模型已达到接近全量数据训练的效果。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力条件生成优于简单的通道拼接，适用于提高降水降尺度的概率真实性；预训练基础模型表征在数据稀缺场景下具有显著优势，有助于减少训练数据依赖并保持高性能。

Abstract: High-resolution precipitation fields are essential for hydrological impact assessment, yet global climate model outputs are too coarse and biased for direct use. AI-based statistical downscaling with diffusion models offers a promising approach, but the mechanism by which large-scale atmospheric predictors condition generation remains largely unexplored. We investigate three conditioning strategies for a denoising diffusion probabilistic model applied to daily precipitation downscaling: channel concatenation of upsampled coarse predictors, cross-attention conditioning with a learned convolutional encoder, and cross-attention conditioning with the frozen encoder of the pretrained Prithvi WxC weather foundation model. All strategies are evaluated against an unconditioned baseline under identical conditions using probabilistic, distributional, spectral, and extreme-event metrics for the Colorado River Basin. Concatenation conditioning achieves the lowest point-wise CRPS and MSE, but tends to produce over-smoothed fields that suppress high-intensity events. In contrast, cross-attention conditioning provides substantially better distributional realism and modest improvements in spectral fidelity. Improvements are greatest for extremes: the Prithvi-WxC conditioned model retains over half of >100mm/day events, although estimates are uncertain due to limited samples. When trained on the full dataset, the learned convolutional model performs similarly to the foundation model-conditioned approach while requiring lower computational resources. However, the Prithvi-WxC-conditioned model achieves comparable performance with only five years of training data. These results indicate that cross-attention conditioning offers advantages over simple concatenation for probabilistic precipitation downscaling, and that pre-trained foundation model representations may offer benefits in data-limited settings.

</details>


### [72] [Learning Late, Guiding Early: Timestep-Decoupled Semantic Guidance for Fair Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25862)
*Subir Kumar Parida,Rajbabu Velmurugan,Ketan Kotwal,R. S. Sengar,Swati Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为语义边界预测器（SBP）的推理时框架，通过在反向去噪过程中一次性干预实现人口统计学引导，无需重新训练或修改模型架构。该方法利用不同扩散阶段潜在表示的语义角色差异：后期潜在表示具有更强的人口统计学可分性，而早期潜在表示则更利于语义干预。SBP从后期潜在表示中学习线性语义边界，并仅在初始噪声潜在表示处应用一次，后续去噪过程保持不变。实验表明，在CelebA-HQ数据集上，该方法显著提升了生成图像的人口统计学公平性，性别、二分类种族和四分类种族的公平性差距分别降低98%、95%和15%，同时保持各群体间图像感知质量一致。由于其单次干预策略和模型无关设计，SBP计算开销小，可轻松集成到现有预训练潜空间扩散模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人脸生成中的年龄、性别、种族等人口统计学不平衡问题会传播至下游人脸识别系统，影响公平性。现有公平性导向生成方法通常需要模型重训练、架构修改或反复引导，成本高且不灵活。因此亟需一种高效、轻量、无需额外训练的推理时干预机制来提升生成公平性。

Method: 提出语义边界预测器（SBP），利用扩散过程不同时间步的潜在表示特性：后期潜在表示具备更强的人口统计学区分能力，早期潜在表示更适合语义干预。通过在后期潜在表示中学习线性语义边界，并仅在初始噪声潜在表示处进行一次干预，使后续去噪过程不受干扰，从而在不改变原模型的前提下实现公平性引导。

Result: 在CelebA-HQ数据集上的实验显示，SBP显著减少人口统计学不公平性：性别公平性差距降低98%，二分类种族降低95%，四分类种族降低15%；同时保持各群体生成图像的感知质量一致。该方法计算开销极小，兼容现有预训练潜空间扩散模型，具备良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: SBP是一种高效、模型无关、推理时的公平性引导框架，通过一次性的语义边界干预即可显著提升合成人脸生成的公平性，为扩散模型在公平性敏感场景下的应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Demographic imbalance in synthetic face generation can propagate to downstream face recognition systems, making fairness an important consideration when diffusion models are used for data generation. Existing fairness-aware generation approaches often require model retraining, architectural modifications, or repeated guidance throughout the reverse diffusion process. In this work, we introduce Semantic Boundary Predictor (SBP), an inference-time framework that performs demographic guidance through a one-shot intervention during reverse denoising. Our approach is motivated by the observation that latent representations at different diffusion timesteps play distinct semantic roles: late-stage latents provide stronger demographic separability, whereas early-stage latents offer greater flexibility for semantic intervention. SBP leverages this timestep decoupling by learning linear semantic boundaries from late-stage latent representations while applying them only once at the initial noisy latent, allowing the remainder of the reverse denoising process to proceed unchanged. The method requires neither retraining nor fine-tuning of the underlying Latent Diffusion Model and operates without external balanced datasets. Experiments on CelebA-HQ demonstrate substantial improvements in demographic fairness, reducing fairness disparity by 98% for gender, 95% for binary race, and 15% for four-class race, while maintaining perceptual image quality across demographic groups. Owing to its one-shot inference strategy and model-agnostic design, SBP introduces only a small computational overhead and can be readily integrated with existing pre-trained latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [73] [LUTSeg: A Longitudinal Multi-Expert Dataset for Ulcer Tissue Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25866)
*Karen Sanchez,Carlos Hinojosa,Albert A. Ávila,Andrea C. Riano-Rojas,Diego H. Romero,Jenny C. Páez,Martina Llinás,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LUTSeg数据集和TiSage框架，用于慢性溃疡组织分割，解决标注成本高和多组织数据稀缺问题，在低标签设置下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性溃疡进展监测需要量化组织成分，但像素级标注成本高，且针对麻风等被忽视疾病的数据集稀缺。

Method: 引入LUTSeg数据集，包含141张图像和五类组织标注；提出TiSage框架，结合冻结的医学视觉-语言模型的多尺度语义先验与教师-学生架构进行半监督分割。

Result: 在LUTSeg和DFUTissue数据集上，TiSage在多数低标签设置下优于监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUTSeg为慢性溃疡研究提供高质量基准数据集，TiSage在少样本条件下表现优异，为医疗图像分割提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Quantifying wound tissue composition is essential for monitoring chronic ulcer progression and guiding treatment decisions. However, pixel-level annotations are costly, and multi-tissue wound datasets remain scarce, particularly for neglected diseases such as leprosy. We introduce LUTSeg, a longitudinal chronic ulcer dataset comprising 141 images from 39 patients with wound masks and five tissue categories annotated by five expert clinicians, including a multi-expert gold-standard subset for inter-rater agreement analysis. To establish an initial benchmark for LUTSeg, we further propose TiSage, a semi-supervised tissue segmentation framework that integrates multi-scale semantic priors from a frozen medical vision-language model within a teacher-student architecture. We evaluate TiSage on LUTSeg and DFUTissue, showing improvements over supervised and semi-supervised baselines in most low-label settings. Code & data: https://github.com/carlosh93/TiSage

</details>


### [74] [Embedding NDRE Trajectories into Contrastive Learning for Label-Free, Physiology-Aware Crop-Stress Staging and DSS Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25888)
*Shafqaat Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EigenCL is a physiology-guided contrastive learning framework that stages crop stress from Sentinel-2 NDRE trajectories, enabling interpretable and transferable stress diagnostics for decision support systems. Trained on maize data from Iowa (2020), it generalized to Nebraska (2023) without retraining, outperforming multiple baselines in clustering quality and aligning with physiological growth stages and drought indicators.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional methods like vegetation index thresholds or image-based clustering fail to capture the progression of crop stress, limiting their usefulness in farm decision-making under increasing drought frequency.

Method: EigenCL uses contrastive learning guided by crop physiology to analyze NDRE trajectories from Sentinel-2 data, identifying four physiologically coherent stress clusters (Healthy, Mild, Moderate, Severe) based on temporal dynamics of vegetation signals.

Result: EigenCL achieved high clustering performance (Silhouette = 0.748, DBI = 0.35, CHI = 49,624), correlated strongly with soil moisture (rho up to 0.72) and yield anomalies, and accurately identified severe stress during critical growth stages like tasseling-silking.

Conclusion: By embedding NDRE trajectory dynamics into a physics-informed contrastive learning framework, EigenCL enables early, interpretable, and scalable crop stress monitoring, supporting climate-smart agronomy and decision-making through actionable outputs like heatmaps and risk indices.

Abstract: Timely detection of crop stress is critical for sustaining yields under increasing drought frequency, yet conventional vegetation index thresholds or image-based clustering often fail to capture stress progression, limiting their value for farm decision-making. To address this gap, we present EigenCL, a physiology-guided contrastive learning framework that stages crop stress from Sentinel-2 NDRE trajectories, with the goal of providing interpretable and transferable stress diagnostics for decision support systems (DSS). EigenCL was trained on 10,000 maize NDRE patches from drought-affected Iowa fields in 2020 and tested on Nebraska fields in 2023 without retraining, with validation incorporating soil-moisture records, U.S. Drought Monitor maps, and county-level yield statistics. The model produced four physiologically coherent stress clusters (Healthy, Mild, Moderate, Severe), significantly outperforming baselines including K-Means, SimCLR, ProtoCLR, and an ablation model (Silhouette = 0.748, DBI = 0.35, CHI = 49,624). Clusters aligned with maize growth stages, with severe stress peaking around tasseling-silking (VT-R1), a stage known to drive yield loss; moreover, EigenCL clusters correlated with soil moisture at 0-14-day lags (rho up to 0.72) and matched yield anomalies in drought-affected counties. By embedding NDRE trajectory dynamics into contrastive learning, EigenCL enables early stress alerts and interpretable DSS outputs (e.g., heatmaps, scouting priorities, regional risk indices), extending beyond single-date NDRE thresholds and supporting scalable monitoring for climate-smart agronomy.

</details>


### [75] [Visual General Intelligence: A White Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25924)
*Hirokatsu Kataoka,Yoshihiro Fukuhara,Yonglong Tian,Shangzhe Wu,Oishi Deb,Ryousuke Yamada,Christian Rupprecht,Jianyuan Wang,Kohsuke Ide,Koichi Namekata,Xianzheng Ma,Yiming Chen,Robert Geirhos,Aditi Raghunathan,Yuki M. Asano,Deva Ramanan,David Fouhey,Andrew J. Davison,Yilun Du,Jiajun Wu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从以视觉为中心的视角重新审视智能，探讨视觉经验与学习所催生的智能是否可成为通往通用人工智能（AGI）的路径。通过整合来自不同背景的研究者观点，论文讨论了视觉智能（VGI）在AGI时代的作用，强调视觉模态（如图像、视频、几何）在智能发展中的潜力，并探索视觉与其他模态（如语言）的关系，而非给出单一定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在文本领域展现出强大的泛化能力，但视觉模态是否也能催生类似智能尚不明确。本文旨在探索视觉智能作为通向AGI的可能路径，推动计算机视觉在AGI时代的范式转变。

Method: 通过跨学科、跨机构的多方观点整合，系统分析视觉智能的核心要素，包括输入模态、学习范式、评估基准及与语言等其他模态的交互机制。

Result: 提出视觉一般智能（VGI）作为未来研究方向，强调视觉应作为核心模态之一，在构建通用智能系统中发挥关键作用。

Conclusion: 视觉智能不仅是技术发展的延伸，更是实现通用人工智能的重要途径；未来应以视觉为核心，融合多模态协同，推动智能系统的本质进化。

Abstract: This paper reconsiders intelligence from a vision-centered perspective and examines whether intelligence emerging from visual experience and learning may provide a pathway toward AGI. In the language domain, beginning with the introduction of the Transformer architecture, the GPT series has demonstrated transfer to unseen tasks through autoregressive language modeling on web-scale text combined with aggressive scaling. This raises a natural question, namely, what capabilities and forms of intelligence can emerge from visual modalities such as images, videos, and geometry? In this paper, we discuss whether visual intelligence can serve as a pathway toward AGI, referred to in this paper as visual general intelligence (VGI), by bringing together contributors from diverse standpoints and affiliations. Our aim is not to offer a single definition of visual intelligence, but to clarify the principles that computer vision should pursue in the AGI era, the visual input modalities, the benchmarks, the learning paradigms, and the relationship between vision, when taken as the core, and other modalities such as language.

</details>


### [76] [Code World Model: Coding Agent as World Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25927)
*Yiwen Chen,Guosheng Lin,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Code World Model框架，通过语言模型的推理与编码能力结合视频模型的生成先验，将世界演化与视觉呈现分离。编码代理作为世界大脑，生成可执行代码以维持持久世界状态并进行规则一致的演化；引入代理表示编码时空约束，并生成代理视频以指导视频模型生成高保真视觉观测。基于游戏和真实世界视频构建对齐的代理-观测对，微调后模型能遵循代理定义的时空规范，在简单交互世界中保持丰富的视觉细节与动态。该方法为开放世界建模提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的世界模型主要从视觉观察中学习动态，但视觉仅反映结果而非底层规则与机制，导致难以维持持续后果和实现连贯、开放的演化。

Method: 提出Code World Model框架，利用编码代理作为世界大脑，通过生成可执行代码来维护世界状态并执行规则一致的演化；设计代理表示编码帧级时空约束，编译为代理视频，由视频模型根据此条件生成高保真视觉输出；构建数据管道，从游戏和真实世界视频中提取对齐的代理-观测对进行训练。

Result: 在微调后，MiniMax-H3能够依据代理定义的时空规范，在由编码代理构建的简单交互世界中持续演化，同时保留丰富的视觉细节和动态特性，验证了代码驱动持久演化与视频模型灵活视觉生成相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合代码实现持久世界演化与视频模型实现灵活视觉生成，为构建开放、连贯、可扩展的世界模型提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: World models aim to simulate how complex environments evolve under actions and events, yet existing video-based world models primarily learn dynamics from visual observations, which reveal outcomes rather than the underlying knowledge, rules, and mechanisms governing world evolution. This makes it difficult to maintain persistent consequences and support coherent, open-ended evolution. We introduce Code World Model, a framework that separates world evolution from visual realization by combining the reasoning and coding capabilities of language models with the generative priors of video models. A coding agent serves as the world brain, reasoning about events and their consequences and generating executable code to maintain persistent world state and perform rule-consistent evolution. To connect executable state with visual generation, we introduce a proxy representation that encodes frame-wise spatiotemporal constraints and is compiled into a proxy video, which conditions a video model to render high-fidelity visual observations. We further develop data pipelines for constructing aligned proxy-observation pairs from gameplay and real-world videos. After fine-tuning on paired gameplay data, MiniMax-H3 follows proxy-based spatiotemporal specifications from simple interactive worlds built by the coding agent while preserving rich visual details and dynamics. These results demonstrate the potential of combining code for persistent world evolution with video models for flexible visual realization, providing a new path toward open-ended world models.

</details>


### [77] [When Composition Doesn't Add Up: Humans Identifying Defects in AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25933)
*Ruoqi Hu,Chulin Zhao,Jiashuo Chang,Ramon Ruiz-Dolz,Hanhe Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对文本到图像（T2I）模型在处理包含多重实体和属性的复杂组合提示时存在的系统性缺陷，通过人工筛选651张具有复杂组合特征的参考图像，结合ChatGPT生成并编辑提示词，生成AI图像并开展大规模主观评估。共29名参与者对每张图像进行多标签缺陷标注，最终构建了CO-AID数据集，涵盖参考图像、提示词、生成图像及缺陷类型与位置信息。实验表明，基于CO-AID训练的深度模型可有效预测和优化AI图像生成中的缺陷，验证了数据集的有效性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成模型在面对复杂组合提示（如多个实体、多个属性）时存在系统性缺陷，但缺乏对人类如何识别这些缺陷的深入理解，也缺少专门用于评估和优化此类缺陷的高质量数据集。

Method: 人工选取651张涵盖人物、手部、物体和场景四类的复杂组合图像，基于这些图像生成并编辑提示词；将提示输入三个主流T2I模型生成图像；组织29名参与者对生成图像进行多标签缺陷评估，标注缺陷类型与空间位置；最终构建CO-AID数据集。

Result: 构建的CO-AID数据集能够支持缺陷预测与图像生成优化，训练的深度模型在预测缺陷和提升生成质量方面表现良好，证明了数据集的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建首个专注于复杂组合缺陷的主观评估数据集CO-AID，揭示了当前T2I模型在复杂提示下的主要缺陷模式，并展示了利用该数据集进行缺陷检测与生成优化的可行性，为未来图像生成模型的评估与改进提供了重要基础。

Abstract: *Chulin Zhao and Ruoqi Hu contributed equally to this work.
  State-of-the-art text-to-image (T2I) models exhibit pronounced and systematic defects when prompts involve intricate compositional factors such as multiple entities and multiple attributes. In this paper, we investigate how humans identify such defects. Specifically, we manually select 651 reference images from the four categories of people, hand, object, and scene that exhibit complex compositional characteristics, from which prompts emphasizing compositional factors are derived by manually editing ChatGPT-generated prompts. We then feed the prompts into three selected T2I models to generate AI images and conduct a comprehensive subjective study to identify their defects. For each image, 29 participants provide multi-label assessments specifying defect types and locations. The study yields the compositional AI-generated image defect (CO-AID) dataset, including reference images, prompts, AI-generated images, and information on defect locations and types. Experimental results show that training a deep model on CO-AID can both predict defects in AI-generated images and optimize AI image generation, demonstrating its usability and effectiveness. The database and supplementary materials are available at: https://github.com/Future-IQA/CO-AID .

</details>


### [78] [TAU-Agent: An Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Traffic Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25935)
*Yuqiang Lin,Yan Shi,Sam Lockyer,Harish Tayyar Madabushi,Adrian Evans,Wenbin Li,Yinhai Wang,Nic Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAU-Agent 是一个用于交通异常理解的代理检索增强框架，通过视频描述工具和开放词汇追踪工具检索相关证据，并结合视觉语言模型进行推理与回答生成。在 AI City Challenge 2026 的多个赛道中表现优异，分别获得第二、第十二和第五名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通异常理解需要模型不仅能检测异常，还需能推理并解释交通视频中的异常事件，现有方法在证据获取与多模态推理方面存在不足。

Method: 提出 TAU-Agent 框架，由中央检索代理协调视频描述工具和开放词汇追踪工具，提取与查询相关的视频片段、对象轨迹等证据，再输入到微调过的视觉语言模型中完成最终推理与答案生成。

Result: 在 AI City Challenge 2026 的 Track 3、Track 7 和 Track 8 上分别取得 0.6779、0.3998 和 67.9275 的成绩，排名第二、第十二和第五。

Conclusion: TAU-Agent 通过检索增强与多工具协同机制，在交通异常理解任务中展现出强大的性能，具备良好的可扩展性与实用性。

Abstract: Traffic Anomaly Understanding (TAU) requires models and systems to detect, reason about, and explain anomalous events in transportation videos. To address this challenge, we propose TAU-Agent, an agentic retrieval-augmented framework for traffic anomaly understanding. Given a task query, a central retrieval agent orchestrates two visual perception tools, namely a Video Captioning Tool and an Open-Vocabulary Tracking Tool, to retrieve and select query-relevant evidence, including captions, temporal intervals, and object trajectories. The selected evidence, together with sampled video frames and the input query, is provided to a supervised fine-tuned vision-language model for final reasoning and answer generation. We evaluate TAU-Agent on both the in-domain and the out-of-domain benchmarks from the AI City Challenge 2026. TAU-Agent achieves scores of 0.6779 on Track 3, 0.3998 on Track 7, and 67.9275 on Track 8, ranking second, twelfth, and fifth, respectively. Code is available at: https://github.com/siri-rouser/TAU-Agent.

</details>


### [79] [Auditable CT Phenotyping Through Report-derived Radiological Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25948)
*Riga Wu,Walter Witschey,Yicheng Li,Felix Barajas Ordonez,Keno K. Bressem,Lisa C. Adams,Gary E. Weissman,Li Shen,Christos Davatzikos,Eduardo Barbosa,Daniel Truhn,Tianyu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种可审计的CT表型预测方法（ACT），基于报告中提取的放射学观察，评估了医学影像基础模型在221个电子健康记录（EHR）表型上的表现。ACT在零样本和线性探测任务中均优于多个视觉-语言基线模型，但分析发现其准确率背后存在大量非病理相关性的“捷径”信号，如某些描述主动脉和冠状动脉钙化的短语在20个不同表型中排名第一。通过限制观察库为临床医生指定的有效证据，可在不损失准确率的情况下使模型聚焦于与表型相关的观察，表明当前模型可能依赖非有效证据进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像基础模型虽能高精度预测临床表型，但其决策机制是否基于真实的疾病特征尚不清楚，可能存在利用与诊断相关但无实际病理意义的‘捷径’现象。本研究旨在揭示并验证这些捷径的存在，并提供可审计的方法来识别和干预无效预测信号。

Method: 构建基于报告衍生放射学观察的可审计CT表型预测框架（ACT），使用38,317名患者数据训练，挖掘376,194条观察，对25,183名未见患者进行评估；采用零样本标注和线性探测两种方式比较性能；通过分析每个表型的最高排名观察，识别捷径信号；进一步限定观察池为临床医生确认的有效证据，重新评估模型表现。

Result: ACT在221个表型上超越五种视觉-语言基线，在零样本评分（0.651 vs 0.572）和线性探测（0.709 vs 0.662）中均表现更优；然而仅97个观察占据所有表型的排名首位，其中一条关于钙化的短语在20个无关表型中排名第一；将观察库限制为临床医生指定证据后，模型在86个表型中成功转向相关观察，且准确率几乎不变（0.751 vs 0.741）。

Conclusion: 尽管当前模型在预测上表现出色，但其准确性可能依赖于非病理相关的捷径信号；通过引入临床可审计机制，可有效识别并纠正这些误导性线索，提升模型的可解释性和临床可信度，推动更可靠的医学影像辅助诊断系统发展。

Abstract: Medical image foundation models can predict clinical phenotypes from computed tomography (CT), but strong performance leaves open whether they read disease-specific findings or shortcuts that correlate with the diagnosis. We tested this in 221 electronic-health-record (EHR) phenotypes using Auditable CT phenotyping (ACT), built on report-derived radiological observations. We trained ACT on 38,317 patients, mined 376,194 observations and evaluated it in 25,183 held-out patients. ACT exceeded five vision-language baselines on zero-shot annotation, and CT-CLIP across 221 phenotypes from unseen CT pulmonary angiography, both under zero-shot scoring (0.651 versus 0.572) and under linear probing (0.709 versus 0.662). Reading each probe exposes what accuracy conceals: only 97 observations occupy the 221 rank-1 positions, and one phrase describing aortic and coronary calcification ranks first for 20 phenotypes, including osteoporosis, urinary tract infection and major depressive disorder. Restricting the bank to clinician-specified evidence redirects those probes onto phenotype-related observations in 86 phenotypes at no accuracy cost (0.751 versus 0.741). Accurate CT-based EHR phenotyping can therefore rest on observations that are not valid evidence for the coded phenotype and that ACT can identify and intervene on.

</details>


### [80] [4DGS-WAM: Bridging Past and Future with an Object-Centric World Action Model based on 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25956)
*Yueen Ma,Zenglin Xu,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于4D高斯点云的显式世界动作模型（4DGS-WAM），通过分离动态物体与静态背景，实现对动态对象的高效预测和静态内容的复用。该方法利用4D高斯点云表示，在保持空间结构的同时避免重复处理冗余背景信息，提升未来状态预测效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型多基于2D视觉数据，虽视觉质量高但缺乏显式空间结构且重复处理背景；而点云虽具3D表达能力却难以跨视角对齐与累积。因此需要一种能保留动态物体与静态背景分离结构、支持长期复用的4D表示方法。

Method: 采用4D高斯点云（4DGS）表示，分别建模动态物体与静态背景。使用策略模型预测未来动作，世界模型预测物体高斯点云的变换；静态背景无需重生成，直接复用历史帧信息，形成以物体为中心的世界动作模型。

Result: 在KITTI-MOT数据集上的实验表明，4DGS-WAM在短时预测和历史重建任务中表现优异，有效提升了预测精度并减少了冗余计算。

Conclusion: 4DGS-WAM通过显式4D表示实现了动态物体与静态背景的解耦建模，显著提升了未来状态预测的效率与准确性，为复杂场景下的世界建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current world action models (WAMs) typically operate on 2D visual data. These models can achieve exceptional visual quality, but they lack explicit spatial structure for individual objects and repeatedly process redundant background content. Although point clouds can represent the world in 3D space, they can be difficult to align and accumulate across viewpoints. In this paper, we leverage an explicit 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) representation that separately models dynamic objects and the static background of a scene. For dynamic objects, we use a policy model to predict future actor actions and a world model to predict transformations of their observed Gaussian splats. The static background need not be regenerated for future states, as much of it has already been observed in past frames. This forms an object-centric world action model, which we name 4DGS-WAM. It lifts 2D observations into a persistent 4D representation so that previously observed static content can be reused during future prediction. Future-state extrapolation can then focus on modeling the evolution of dynamic objects. Experiments on KITTI-MOT evaluate short-horizon prediction and past reconstruction.

</details>


### [81] [Less Contouring, More Accuracy: Lesion-Guided ROI Deep Learning for Ovarian Ultrasound Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25965)
*Mehran Ahmad,Ali Abbasian Ardakani,Afshin Mohammadi,Alisa Mohebbi,Gernot Kronreif,Sepideh Hatamikia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于病灶引导的感兴趣区域（ROI）深度学习在卵巢超声图像分类中的应用，旨在降低像素级分割标注负担的同时保持高诊断性能。在两个公开数据集（MMOTU和OUD）上对比了四种策略：全局图像深度学习、病灶引导ROI深度学习、病灶轮廓深度学习以及基于轮廓的放射组学方法。结果表明，病灶引导ROI策略表现最优，其中MaxViT-Tiny模型在MMOTU数据集上达到93.10%准确率和0.99 AUC，在OUD数据集上达到97.56%准确率和0.99 AUC，显著优于其他方法且标注成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卵巢病变超声分类因影像特征重叠及依赖专家判断而具有挑战性。现有深度学习方法通常需要大量像素级标注，导致标注成本高。本研究旨在通过引入病灶引导的感兴趣区域（ROI）策略，减少标注负担，同时保持或提升诊断性能，推动可扩展的AI辅助超声分析。

Method: 采用四类策略：全局图像深度学习、病灶引导ROI深度学习、病灶轮廓深度学习、轮廓基放射组学+机器学习分类器。使用四种深度学习模型（MaxViT-Tiny, Swin Transformer, EfficientNet-B7, ResNet18），并在低样本量的OUD数据集上结合ANOVA特征选择与SVM、KNN、ANN等分类器构建放射组学模型。所有方法在统一框架下进行比较评估。

Result: 病灶引导ROI策略表现最佳：MaxViT-Tiny在MMOTU数据集上实现93.10%准确率和0.99 AUC；在OUD数据集上达到97.56%准确率和0.99 AUC。轮廓基方法虽性能相近，但需更高标注成本。整体表明该策略在性能与效率之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 病灶引导的ROI深度学习能够在保证高诊断准确性的同时显著降低标注需求，是实现可扩展、高效且实用的AI辅助卵巢超声分析的有效途径。

Abstract: Ovarian lesion classification using transvaginal ultrasound remains challenging due to overlapping imaging characteristics and the dependence on expert interpretation. This study investigates whether lesion-guided region-of-interest (ROI) deep learning can achieve competitive diagnostic performance while reducing the annotation burden associated with pixel-level lesion segmentation. Two publicly available ovarian ultrasound datasets were evaluated: the Multi-Modality Ovarian Tumor Ultrasound (MMOTU) dataset for eight-class classification and the Ovarian Ultrasound Dataset (OUD) for binary classification. Four strategies were compared under a unified framework: global image-based deep learning, lesion-guided ROI-based deep learning, lesion contour-based deep learning, and contour-based radiomics with machine learning classifiers. Four deep learning architectures, MaxViT-Tiny, Swin Transformer, EfficientNet-B7, and ResNet18, were evaluated. Radiomics models were developed using support vector machine, k-nearest neighbors, and artificial neural network classifiers, with ANOVA-based feature selection applied for the lower-sample OUD dataset. The lesion-guided ROI strategy achieved the strongest overall performance, with MaxViT-Tiny obtaining 93.10% accuracy and an AUC of 0.99 on MMOTU and 97.56% accuracy and an AUC of 0.99 on OUD. The contour-based approach achieved comparable accuracy but required substantially higher annotation effort. These findings demonstrate that lesion-guided ROI deep learning provides an effective balance between diagnostic performance and annotation efficiency, offering a practical approach for scalable AI-assisted ovarian ultrasound analysis

</details>


### [82] [PANDA - Prototype-Anchored Alignment for Partially Unpaired Multimodal Learning, with Applications to Alzheimers MRI and TCGA Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25970)
*Sheethal Bhat,Mahfuzur Rahman Chowdhury,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Stephan Wunderlich,Rose Dawn Bharat,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PANDA是一种两阶段框架，用于在辅助模态与主模态不完全配对的情况下，将辅助信息迁移至主模态模型，且推理时无需辅助输入。第一阶段从配对子集学习共享嵌入并估计类别原型；第二阶段在所有受试者上训练主编码器，通过交叉熵和对冻结原型的对齐来优化。该方法适应任意配对率，包括零重叠情况。在ADNI和TCGA-Lung数据集上的实验表明，PANDA显著提升预测性能，尤其在低配对率下仍保持稳定，且无需推理时依赖辅助数据，具有部署友好性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模态医学预测中，辅助模态（如影像、基因、表型）常仅部分可用或无重叠，而传统方法依赖完整配对，限制了实际应用。需要一种能在不完整配对条件下有效利用辅助信息的方法，以提高主模态模型的预测能力。

Method: PANDA采用两阶段策略：1）基于配对数据学习共享嵌入，并从辅助模态中估计类别原型；2）使用冻结的原型作为监督信号，训练主模态编码器，实现跨模态知识迁移，且推理时不需辅助输入。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上，相对于仅用MRI的基线，PANDA在不同配对率下均显著提升分类性能（如AUC达0.893），减少假阳性；在TCGA-Lung数据集上，生存预测性能优于仅用WSI的模型，且优于全融合训练方式，尽管统计显著性受限于小样本。

Conclusion: PANDA提供了一种适用于不完整配对场景的高效多模态学习机制，可在无需辅助模态输入的前提下，有效提升主模态预测性能，具备强部署实用性。

Abstract: Multimodal medical prediction often faces incomplete pairing: auxiliary modalities with complementary signal are available for only a subset of subjects (or none) and cannot be assumed at deployment. We introduce PANDA (Prototype Anchored Data Alignment), a two-stage framework that transfers auxiliary information to a primary-modality model without auxiliary inputs at inference. Stage 1 learns a shared embedding from the paired subset and estimates class prototypes from auxiliary modalities; Stage 2 trains the primary encoder on all subjects using cross-entropy plus alignment to the frozen prototypes. Because supervision is defined at the class-prototype level, PANDA accommodates arbitrary pairing rates, including zero subject overlap. We evaluate PANDA on two applications. On a 1,021-subject multi-scanner ADNI cohort, we perform AD/CN classification with three auxiliary modalities at distinct pairing rates: tabular scores (44.8%), FDG-PET (18.7%), and external handwriting kinematics (0% overlap). Relative to the same-backbone MRI-only baseline, PANDA attains AUC 0.868 +-0.020 (+7.9pp) and reduces 1.5T CN false positives by 24.3pp; on a fully trainable Conv5-FC3 backbone it reaches AUC 0.893 (best overall). A pairing-rate ablation shows that the joint anchor remains within seed noise from 75% to 5% pairing. On TCGA-Lung survival prediction from whole-slide images with RNA-seq as auxiliary data, PANDA improves over WSI-only on 2-year OS (AUC +3.5pp) and Cox PH (C-index +9.0pts) and outperforms full-fusion training, which underperforms WSI-only, while requiring no RNA at inference; wide confidence intervals on this smaller cohort keep the gains below conventional significance. Overall, PANDA provides a deployment-oriented mechanism for leveraging incomplete auxiliary modalities to improve primary-modality prediction.

</details>


### [83] [FRAME: separating sampling variation from representational cause in medical imaging fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25981)
*Mahshad Lotfinia,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FRAME is a two-step framework for auditing fairness in medical imaging models. It establishes a fair-model reference to account for differences due to subgroup sizes and tests remaining disparities using representation space operators. Results show that much of observed race/age differences can be explained by sampling variation, while interventions like demographic decodability or entanglement have minimal impact on within-group rankings. Image-text pretraining improves worst-group performance. FRAME helps distinguish meaningful bias from statistical noise.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address the challenge of distinguishing true fairness bias from sampling variation in medical imaging models, especially when subgroup performance differences are used as evidence of bias.

Method: FRAME uses a two-step approach: (1) deriving a fair-model reference based on subgroup sizes under exact fairness; (2) testing residual differences via operators in representation space that preserve within-group rankings. Interventions include manipulating demographic encoding and group-disease entanglement.

Result: The fair-model reference explains 41% of race differences and 22% of age differences across 702,206 images and 36 encoders. Remaining differences are insensitive to interventions beyond random seed variation. In 9 published studies, the reference accounts for 25% of rate differences and 70% of AUC differences. Image-text pretraining boosts worst-group performance by ~0.05.

Conclusion: FRAME enables more accurate assessment of fairness in medical imaging by separating genuine bias from statistical variation. It helps prioritize interventions that truly improve fairness rather than masking underlying issues.

Abstract: Subgroup performance differences are the standard evidence for fairness bias in medical imaging, and the usual response removes the demographic information that a model encodes. Here we introduce Fair-model Reference And Mechanism Evaluation (FRAME), a two-step framework for auditing such a claim. The first step derives a fair-model reference, the distribution of the difference under exact fairness at the observed subgroup sizes. In the second step, we test the remainder with two operators in representation space. One operator cannot change a within-group ranking by construction. Across 702,206 images and 36 encoders, the reference accounts for a median 41% of the reported race difference and 22% of the age difference. Injecting demographic decodability leaves the remainder unchanged, while entangling the group with the disease direction raises the race difference from 0.077 to 0.118. No intervention we tested changes the remainder more than a change of random seed does. Those interventions reduce a difference at the operating point and leave the within-group ranking difference at a median of 0.000. Applied to 89 differences in 9 published studies across 6 medical imaging modalities, the reference accounts for a median 25% of a rate difference and 70% of a difference in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve. Image-text pretraining instead raises worst-group performance by about 0.05. Applying FRAME before choosing an intervention could distinguish differences that need a mechanistic explanation from differences compatible with sampling variation at the current cohort sizes.

</details>


### [84] [Uncertainty-Guided Latent Diffusion Models for Faithful Super Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25998)
*Ren Wang,Yung-Yu Chuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UGDiff提出一种新的扩散引导范式，通过估计高保真图像对应的潜在特征的重建不确定性，指导扩散过程在高不确定性区域选择性地恢复高频细节，同时在其他区域保持保真度。该方法还自适应地结合估计不确定性与扩散采样器在每一步的后验方差，降低对高保真图像的依赖，从而实现更好的感知-失真平衡。实验表明，该方法优于现有最先进的基于扩散的超分辨率方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图超分辨率中的感知-失真权衡是一个根本挑战。尽管基于扩散的方法在生成感知真实感图像方面表现优异，但在保持高保真度方面仍存在局限。现有方法往往因过度依赖高保真图像而牺牲感知质量，因此需要一种新方法来更好地平衡两者。

Method: 提出UGDiff，通过估计高保真图像潜在特征的重建不确定性，并利用该不确定性引导扩散过程，在高不确定性区域优先恢复高频细节；同时结合扩散采样器的后验方差，自适应识别高不确定性区域，减少后期采样对高保真图像的依赖。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，UGDiff在感知质量和保真度之间实现了更优的平衡，性能优于当前最先进的基于扩散的超分辨率方法。

Conclusion: UGDiff通过引入不确定性引导机制和自适应区域识别策略，有效缓解了扩散模型在超分辨率任务中感知与保真之间的矛盾，为高质量图像重建提供了新思路。

Abstract: The perception-distortion trade-off poses a fundamental challenge in single-image super-resolution (SR). Although diffusion-based SR methods excel at generating perceptually realistic images, achieving high fidelity remains a key limitation. Recent advances in diffusion-based SR have shown promise in improving fidelity, but these methods often compromise perceptual quality due to their high reliance on a high-fidelity image. To address this, we introduce UGDiff, a novel diffusion guidance paradigm designed to further improve the perception-distortion balance. In particular, we first estimate the reconstruction uncertainty of the latent features corresponding to a high-fidelity image. This uncertainty is then used to guide the diffusion process to selectively restore high-frequency details in high-uncertainty regions, while preserving fidelity elsewhere. Furthermore, our guidance method adaptively identifies the high-uncertainty regions by considering not only the estimated uncertainty but also the posterior variance of the diffusion sampler at each timestep. This relaxes the reliance on the high-fidelity image in the later stages of sampling, thereby achieving a better perception-distortion balance. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our method performs favorably against state-of-the-art diffusion-based SR methods.

</details>


### [85] [UltraPIPS: Improving model perception in B-mode ultrasound with foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26033)
*Tal Grutman,Tali Ilovitsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在超声成像中使用LPIPS度量感知相似性的问题，发现自然图像或通用医学图像预训练的主干网络在超声图像上表现不佳，提出应采用针对超声数据设计的专用模型以更准确地衡量感知相似性。实验表明，使用超声领域专用主干网络的LPIPS在分类、分割和重建等下游任务中与模型性能相关性更强，且优化时能更好平衡重建质量与真实感。代码已开源，推出UltraPIPS库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声图像具有独特的斑点模式和声学特性，与自然图像及其他医学影像差异显著，因此现有基于自然图像预训练的LPIPS模型可能不适用于超声图像的感知相似性评估，亟需开发领域特定的度量方法。

Method: 比较了基于自然图像、医学通用模型和超声专用模型的LPIPS在分类、分割和重建等任务中的表现，评估不同主干网络对下游性能的相关性，并通过优化LPIPS损失验证超声主干的优势。

Result: 超声专用主干模型的LPIPS与下游任务性能相关性更高，且在重建任务中能实现更好的质量与真实感平衡；所提出的UltraPIPS库提供了基于开放源码基础模型的LPIPS度量工具。

Conclusion: 在超声医学影像中，应优先采用领域特定的主干模型来计算LPIPS，以提升感知相似性度量的有效性，该选择对下游任务性能有显著影响。

Abstract: In medical imaging, it is common to use learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS) to compare images semantically in feature space. Although backbones pretrained on natural images are widely used for LPIPS computation, B-mode ultrasound images possess distinct speckle patterns and acoustic-specific image statistics that are fundamentally different from natural images and even from other images in radiology. Consequently, we propose that domain-specific models are needed to measure perceptual similarity in ultrasound data, a finding which is not necessarily the case for other imaging modalities. We compare LPIPS metrics across downstream tasks like classification, segmentation and reconstruction using natural image, medical generalist and ultrasound backbone models and show that selection of LPIPS backbone is a non-trivial design choice. In particular, the ultrasound backbone models were more correlated with downstream performance of supervised models than classical and natural image models, and optimization of the LPIPS loss with an ultrasound backbone achieved a strong balance between reconstruction quality and realism. Our code is available at https://github.com/talg2324/UltraPIPS and introduces the UltraPIPS library, a set of LPIPS metrics based on the open-source foundation models analyzed in this paper.

</details>


### [86] [StreamPI: Streaming Multimodal Temporal Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26067)
*Zhe Liu,Jinghua Hou,Yuxiang Lu,Zhenya Yang,Xianzhe Fan,Junwei Luo,Junyi Li,Ruihua Han,Zhi Hou,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamPI 是一种无需额外参数的流式多模态时序建模框架，通过指令锚定的时序建模实现单帧视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的时序推理能力。其核心设计将每个（视觉观测, 语言指令）对视为原子时序单元，利用双向注意力实现跨模态融合，因果注意力保持自回归流式推理，使语言指令成为任务执行中的持久语义锚点。为弥合同步训练与异步部署之间的差距，提出随机间隔流式训练策略，通过设定合理帧间间隔（如每3帧）加速并平滑动作执行，随机化间隔进一步增强对帧时间扰动的鲁棒性，支持实际异步部署。此外，借助大语言模型（LLM）骨干的长度外推能力，StreamPI 可无缝继承预训练单帧权重，支持灵活的单帧与多帧推理。在涵盖记忆依赖和精确感知场景的真实机器人任务及仿真基准 LIBERO 上的实验表明，StreamPI 在多种任务中均优于 pi0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进 VLA 模型（如 pi0.5）采用单帧范式，无法保留历史观测，限制了空间感知精度与长期任务执行能力。为提升模型的时序推理能力，同时避免引入额外参数，需设计一种轻量级、高效且适用于真实机器人异步部署的流式时序建模方法。

Method: 提出 StreamPI 框架，核心包括：1）指令锚定的时序建模：将（视觉观测, 语言指令）对作为原子时序单元，使用双向注意力进行跨模态融合，因果注意力实现跨对的自回归流式推理；2）随机间隔流式训练策略：在训练中以随机或固定间隔采样帧，提升对帧率变化的鲁棒性；3）利用 LLM 骨干的长度外推能力，实现从单帧到多帧推理的无缝扩展。

Result: StreamPI 在真实机器人任务（含记忆依赖与高精度感知场景）以及仿真基准 LIBERO 上均显著优于 pi0.5，展现出更强的时序推理能力、更高的动作执行效率与更好的鲁棒性，且无需额外参数。

Conclusion: StreamPI 成功为单帧 VLA 模型赋予强大的时序推理能力，无需增加参数量，具备良好的可扩展性与部署适应性，是迈向高效、鲁棒、实用化机器人控制的重要进展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated effectiveness in robot manipulation, yet state-of-the-art models such as pi0.5 operate under a single-frame paradigm, limiting their ability to retain past observations and develop precise spatial perception. In this paper, we propose StreamPI, a streaming multimodal temporal modeling framework that equips single-frame VLA with temporal reasoning capability without introducing any additional parameters. One core design is instruction-anchored temporal modeling. It treats each (visual observation, language instruction) pair as an atomic temporal unit: bidirectional attention within each pair enables cross-modal fusion, while causal attention across pairs preserves autoregressive streaming inference. This ensures the language instruction serves as a persistent semantic anchor throughout task execution. To bridge the gap between synchronous training and asynchronous real-robot deployment, we introduce a andom-interval streaming training strategy: a proper inter-frame interval (e.g., every 3 frames) enables faster and smoother action execution. Beyond this, randomizing the interval further improves robustness to frame-timing perturbations, supporting asynchronous deployment in practice. Furthermore, by leveraging the length extrapolation capability of the LLM backbone, StreamPI seamlessly inherits pretrained single-frame weights and supports flexible single-frame and multi-frame inference. Experiments on real-robot tasks spanning memory-dependent and precise perception scenarios, as well as the simulation benchmark LIBERO, demonstrate that StreamPI outperforms pi0.5 across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [87] [MyoMechanix: Biomechanically-Grounded Compositional Skilled Activity Understanding and Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26094)
*Hao Yin,Paritosh Parmar,Lijun Gu,Lin Xu,Tianxiao Guo,Xiujin Liu,Tianyou Zheng,Yang Zhang,Weiwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MyoMechanix，一个用于负重动作的多模态生态系统，包含7500+样本，同步多视角RGB视频、3D姿态、表面肌电（sEMG）及生理信号，构建了当前最大的多模态动作质量评估基准。引入健身知识图谱（FKG）实现动作、阶段、关键步骤、错误与纠正反馈的结构化组织，支持组合评分和可解释评估。基于此，开发CUBIST模型，通过分解-分析-重构实现细粒度错误归因与反馈生成。还建立了MyoMechanix-AQA、MyoMechanix-VideoQA和新型MyoMechanix-Video2EMG任务。实验表明，多模态感知与结构化表示显著提升性能、可解释性与错误定位能力，CUBIST达到先进水平；VideoQA增强语言引导的动作理解；Video2EMG显示视频可替代高成本肌电传感。该工作推动物理人工智能在健身、康复、医疗等领域的生物力学驱动、多模态与组合推理发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作质量评估数据集和方法主要依赖视觉输入（如RGB和姿态），忽视肌肉力学等生理动态，且将动作视为整体模式，难以提供细粒度、生物力学基础的反馈。因此亟需一种融合运动与肌肉活动、支持精细分析与可解释评估的新范式。

Method: 构建MyoMechanix多模态数据集，整合多视角视频、3D姿态、sEMG及其他生理信号；设计健身知识图谱（FKG）对专家标注进行结构化建模；开发CUBIST系统，采用分解-分析-重组策略实现细粒度错误归因与反馈生成；设立AQA、VideoQA及Video2EMG三项任务以验证系统有效性。

Result: 多模态感知与结构化表示显著提升动作质量评估的性能、可解释性和错误归因精度；CUBIST在多个指标上达到当前最优；VideoQA有效增强语言与动作的对齐理解；Video2EMG任务证明仅从视频即可预测肌电信号，为低成本生理监测提供新路径。

Conclusion: MyoMechanix推动动作质量评估向生物力学驱动、多模态融合与组合推理演进，为物理人工智能在健身、康复、医疗和机器学习中的应用奠定坚实基础。

Abstract: Existing action quality assessment (AQA) datasets and methods rely primarily on visual inputs such as RGB and pose, overlooking physiological dynamics such as muscle mechanics and often modeling actions as monolithic patterns. These limitations hinder fine-grained, biomechanically grounded feedback. We introduce MyoMechanix, a multimodal ecosystem for weight-loaded actions that aligns motion with muscle activity. Expert-annotated, it contains 7,500+ samples of 20 actions from 38 subjects, with synchronized multiview RGB video, 3D pose, sEMG, and additional physiological signals, forming the largest multimodal AQA benchmark to date. We further construct the Fitness Knowledge Graph (FKG), which organizes expert annotations into structured relationships among actions, phases, key steps, errors, and corrective feedback, enabling compositional scoring and interpretable assessment. Building on these representations, we develop CUBIST (Compositional Ontological Reasoning Engine), which performs decomposition-analysis-recomposition for fine-grained error attribution and feedback generation. We also establish MyoMechanix-AQA, MyoMechanix-VideoQA, and a novel MyoMechanix-Video2EMG task. Experiments show that multimodal sensing and structured representations improve performance, interpretability, and error attribution, with CUBIST achieving state-of-the-art results; VideoQA enhances language-grounded action understanding; and Video2EMG suggests video-based alternatives to costly EMG sensing. MyoMechanix advances skilled activity understanding toward biomechanically grounded, multimodal, and compositional reasoning for Physical AI applications in fitness, rehabilitation, healthcare, and machine learning. Project page: https://haoyin116.github.io/MyoMechanix/

</details>


### [88] [A Visual Dependence-Aware Framework for Multimodal Unsupervised Continual Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26095)
*Kaichen Li,Zhilin Zhu,Jianhao Huang,Zhengqin Lai,Baochen Xiong,Zibo Shao,Yaguang Song,Linhui Xiao,Xiaoshan Yang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态无监督持续后训练任务（MU-CPT），旨在让已部署的多模态大模型从流式无标签数据中持续进化。针对现有方法忽略词元级视觉依赖性（VD）的问题，作者揭示了VD在MU-CPT中的关键作用：其结构失真可指示跨模态灾难性遗忘，而内在异质性则可引导新任务学习。为此，提出VDA框架，包含两个核心组件：1）视觉约束最优传输（VC-OT），将旧任务VD的结构失真建模为最优传输问题，通过区域感知代价和依赖分层惩罚项，防止视觉关注全局偏移并抑制视觉依赖退化为语言偏差；2）视觉调制适应（VMA），利用VD的异质性强化视觉锚定的新任务学习，提升新任务可塑性。实验表明，该方法在保持旧任务稳定性的同时显著增强新任务适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督后训练方法对目标词元进行均匀优化，忽略了词元间的视觉依赖性（VD）差异。然而，研究发现词元级VD在持续学习中至关重要：其结构失真可反映跨模态灾难性遗忘，其异质性可作为新任务学习的引导信号。因此，亟需一种能感知并利用视觉依赖性的方法来实现稳定且高效的多模态持续学习。

Method: 提出Visual Dependence-Aware（VDA）框架，包含两项关键技术：(1) Visually Constrained Optimal Transport（VC-OT），将旧任务视觉依赖结构失真建模为最优传输问题，采用区域感知地面代价与依赖分层传输惩罚，防止视觉注意力漂移与语言偏见；(2) Visually Modulated Adaptation（VMA），基于视觉依赖的异质性动态调节学习过程，增强新任务的视觉锚定与可塑性。

Result: 在自建的MU-CPT设置下，VDA框架在多个基准上显著优于现有方法，有效缓解了跨模态灾难性遗忘，同时实现了旧任务稳定性与新任务学习能力的双重提升。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了词元级视觉依赖性在多模态持续学习中的核心价值，并提出了一个基于视觉依赖感知的高效框架（VDA），成功实现了旧知识保持与新知识获取之间的平衡，为多模态大模型的持续演化提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore a novel task of Multimodal Unsupervised Continual Post-Training (MU-CPT), enabling deployed MLLMs to continually evolve from streaming unlabeled data. Existing unsupervised post-training methods for MLLMs typically optimize target tokens uniformly, overlooking their heterogeneous visual dependence (VD). However, we reveal that token-level VD is crucial for MU-CPT. Specifically, its structural distortion serves as an indicator of cross-modal catastrophic forgetting, and its inherent heterogeneity acts as a compass to guide new-task learning. Leveraging this property, we propose a Visual Dependence-Aware (VDA) framework with two main components. First, Visually Constrained Optimal Transport (VC-OT) formulates the VD structural distortion of old-task VD during new-task learning as an optimal transport problem to mitigate cross-modal forgetting. By designing a region-aware ground cost and a dependence-stratified transport penalty, it prevents global shifts in visual focus while strictly prohibiting visual reliance from degenerating into language bias. Second, Visually Modulated Adaptation (VMA) exploits VD heterogeneity to emphasize visually grounded new-task learning, promoting new-task plasticity. Together, our method simultaneously maintains old-task stability and new-task plasticity during challenging MU-CPT. Extensive experiments under our MU-CPT setting validate the effectiveness of VDA.

</details>


### [89] [RefVideo-6M: A Reliable Reference-Based Dataset for Instructional Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26101)
*Bojia Zi,Xiaoyan Yang,Yu Zhou,Ruijie Sun,Lihan Zhang,Bin Liang,Kam-Fai Wong,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RefVideo-6M，一个大规模参考引导视频编辑数据集，包含500万视频和100万图像编辑样本，通过真实无伪影视频作为目标，结合多专家生成高质量输入条件，并提供约600万视觉参考，支持精细视觉对应学习。基于该数据集训练的Ref-MoT模型验证了其在监督可靠性、视觉质量、可控性和参考一致性方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑数据集存在两大问题：一是目标视频由自动编辑模型生成，可能引入可见伪影和不可靠的监督信号；二是主要依赖文本指令，缺乏对精确、身份保持和可控制编辑至关重要的视觉参考。

Method: 采用构建管道，以无伪影的真实视频作为编辑目标，利用多编辑专家生成经过质量过滤的输入条件，并提供多样化的视觉参考，覆盖多种参考类型和编辑场景，以实现超越仅文本指令的细粒度视觉对应学习。

Result: RefVideo-6M提供了比现有数据集更可靠的监督信号，支持训练出具有更高视觉质量、更强可控性和更好参考一致性的强大编辑模型。实验证明该数据集有效提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: RefVideo-6M是一个高质量、大规模的参考引导视频编辑数据集，显著改善了视频编辑任务中的监督质量和模型表现，为未来研究提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in video editing have been largely driven by large-scale instruction-based datasets. However, existing datasets still suffer from two critical limitations. First, target videos are commonly produced by automatic editing models, which may introduce visible artifacts and unreliable supervision signals. Second, most public datasets rely primarily on textual instructions, while lacking visual references that are crucial for precise, identity-preserving, and controllable editing. To address these limitations, we introduce RefVideo-6M, a large-scale reference-guided editing dataset containing 5 million video editing samples and 1 million image editing samples. To ensure reliable supervision, our dataset uses a construction pipeline that treats artifact-free real videos as editing targets and generates quality-filtered input conditions with multiple editing experts. In addition, it provides approximately 6 million visual references, covering diverse reference types and editing scenarios, thereby enabling models to learn fine-grained visual correspondence beyond text-only instructions. Based on RefVideo-6M, we further train a reference-guided video editing model, Ref-MoT, to evaluate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RefVideo-6M provides substantially more reliable supervision than existing datasets and enables the training of powerful editing models with improved visual quality, controllability, and reference consistency. The open-source dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/RefVideo6M/RefVideo6M.

</details>


### [90] [VBVR-Pro: A Scalable and Verifiable Suite for Native Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26105)
*Junxiang Xu,Ruisi Wang,Fanyi Pu,Maijunxian Wang,Ran Ji,Tongxi Zhou,Chenyang Gu,Jing Zuo,Hongcan Xiao,Yimeng Geng,Wanqi Yin,Wei Chen,Oscar Qian,Zhengan Yan,Ziqi Huang,Haiwen Diao,Liang Pan,Bo Li,Xiangyu Fan,Dezhi Luo,Fengyuan Yu,Zehong Zhao,Qingying Gao,Tinghui Zhu,Yilan Zhang,Jingqi Tong,Pinyuan Feng,Zhengze Jiang,Letian Wang,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Jieneng Chen,Sonia Joseph,Constantin Venhoff,Saman Motamed,Mengyue Yang,Chandra Sripada,Alan Yuille,Philip Torr,Lvmin Zhang,Vikash Kumar,Daniel Khashabi,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte,Raphaël Millière,Vincent C. Müller,Anyi Rao,Quan Wang,Ziwei Liu,Dahua Lin,Lei Yang,Hokin Deng,Zhongang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VBVR-Pro 是一个闭环测试平台，旨在使基于生成的原生视觉推理可训练、可验证、可优化和实验可控。它通过300个程序生成的任务实现任务扩展；提供基于确定性规则的可验证奖励评分器，优于现有基于模型的评判方法；并通过跨30多种图像、视频及混合生成器的受控实验，揭示视频生成在时空状态追踪中的优势以及原生视觉推理轨迹的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前原生视觉推理受限于缺乏可扩展的训练任务、可靠的反馈机制和不同生成媒介间的可控比较，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了一个包含300个程序生成任务的可控任务空间；设计基于任务特定规则的可验证奖励评分器；进行多模态生成器的受控对比实验，包括图像、视频和混合生成器，并通过消融与探针分析验证视觉原生推理路径的存在。

Result: 模型在VBVR-Pro上训练后展现出对多个外部视觉推理基准（如RISE-Video、MME-CoF-Pro、BabyVision）的强大泛化能力；所提出的评分器与人类判断高度一致，且作为强化学习中的奖励信号表现出更强性能；实验表明视频生成在长期时空状态追踪中最优，而混合生成是计算高效的替代方案；证实了视觉原生推理轨迹的关键作用。

Conclusion: VBVR-Pro 为原生视觉推理提供了系统性的训练与评估框架，推动了视觉生成作为推理介质的可行性，其数据、模型、评分器和代码均已开源。

Abstract: Native visual reasoning treats visual generation as the medium of reasoning itself: visual states (i.e. images and videos) are not merely inputs to be understood or outputs to be rendered, but first-class substrates for problem solving beyond language. Yet progress remains bottlenecked by the lack of scalable training tasks, reliable feedback, and controlled comparisons across generative substrates. In this work, we introduce VBVR-Pro, a closed-loop testbed that makes native visual reasoning through generation trainable, verifiable, optimizable, and experimentally controllable. 1) Task scaling. VBVR-Pro turns visual reasoning into a controlled task space of 300 procedurally generated tasks. Models trained on VBVR-Pro show strong transfer beyond the proposed suite across seven external visual reasoning benchmarks such as RISE-Video, MME-CoF-Pro, and BabyVision. 2) Verifiable rewards. VBVR-Pro provides verifiable reward scorers for task-grounded evaluation. Through a systematic study of leading MLLMs as judges, we identify recurring failure modes of the prevalent VLM-as-a-judge paradigm. In contrast, the proposed scorers are grounded in deterministic, task-specific rules, achieve fine-grained alignment with human judgments. Importantly, they serve as reliable reward signals for large-scale multi-task reinforcement learning and demonstrate stronger post-RL performance across visual reasoning tasks. 3) Mechanism study. VBVR-Pro enables controlled modality studies across more than 30 image, video, and interleaved generators. Our analysis shows that video generation remains strongest for tasks requiring persistent spatiotemporal state tracking, while interleaved generation provides a compute-efficient alternative. Critically, ablations and probing suggest the presence of vision-native trajectories that are crucial to visual reasoning. We release all data, models, scorers, and code.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

### [91] [Detection != Reliable Control: Decodable Empathy Directions Yield at Most Partial Shifts in Automated Empathy Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24901)
*Haoran Jisun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在指令微调的大语言模型中，通过可解码的‘共情’方向（认知性识别与情感共鸣）进行文本操控的有效性。尽管情感共鸣方向在自动化评估中表现一致，但认知层面的表现不一致；且即使在去除表面特征干扰后，仍能解码并改写文本，但实际影响有限（如Qwen仅提升0.29，约自然差距的26%）。此外，认知控制虽在算法上看似无效果，实则因测量工具过粗而无法检测真实差异。研究强调共情类声称需进行测量敏感性验证，避免将检测等同于有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将可解码的共情方向视为因果干预手段，但缺乏对测量工具敏感性和人类感知变化的充分检验，可能导致误判。本文旨在系统评估共情两个维度（认知与情感）在大语言模型中的可操控性与有效性。

Method: 采用三个指令微调的大语言模型（Qwen、Llama、Gemma），基于EPITOME框架分别操纵‘识别’（认知）与‘共鸣’（情感）两个共情维度。使用双LLM评分员与判别式EPITOME分类器进行评估，并设置情绪性与中性正向对照组。通过残差化处理排除句子嵌入表面特征的影响，同时引入域内控制以检验测量工具的灵敏度。

Result: 情感共鸣方向在所有自动化工具中均通过控制测试，但认知维度结果不一致；在残差化后仍可解码，且文本可被显著改写。然而，添加共鸣方向仅部分提升情感得分（如Qwen+0.29，约26%自然差距）；认知控制未产生可测变化，但其原因在于测量工具过于粗糙，非真正无效应。此外，Gemma的识别维度消减会降低分类器得分，说明其具有可测量性。

Conclusion: 检测到信号不等于实现可靠控制。当前对大语言模型中‘共情’方向的操控声称，必须经过严格的测量敏感性检查，否则易导致误导性结论。特别是认知与情感维度应分开评估，避免混淆。

Abstract: A decodable "empathy" direction is routinely read as a causal lever, conflating decodability, automated-metric control, and human-perceived change. We test this for two EPITOME-derived facets -- Recognition (cognitive) and Resonance (affective) -- in three instruction-tuned LLMs, scoring every intervention with two LLM judges and a discriminative EPITOME classifier, each gated by an emotional-vs-neutral positive control. The control passes for the affective facet across all automated instruments, but cognitive range is inconsistent across them. Both facets remain decodable after residualizing against a sentence-embedding-derived surface score, and steering can substantially rewrite the text. Yet adding the Resonance direction raises the affective score only partially -- in Qwen by +0.29 (approximately 26% of the natural gap). A direct between-direction contrast confirms the shift is facet-specific in Qwen and Llama (not Gemma); we do not, however, establish a matching human-perceived change. Additive cognitive steering produces no measurable change, but a within-domain control shows the cognitive instrument is too coarse to resolve the differences such steering would produce -- unmeasurable, not a clean null. By contrast, Gemma Recognition ablation lowers the classifier's cognitive score even after adjusting for response length. Detection does not imply reliable control under global interventions, and cognitive-empathy claims warrant an explicit measurement-sensitivity check.

</details>


### [92] [Semantic Variability of Replies Across LLMs: Implications for Designing Conversation-Based Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24920)
*Jiangang Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了当底层大语言模型（LLM）改变时，LLM生成的回复是否保持语义一致性。通过使用真实协作对话中的消息，比较了在有无先前聊天历史的情况下，不同LLM生成回复的语义相似性。结果表明，模型选择和对话上下文都会影响回复的相似性和与人类回复的一致性。这说明仅靠提示和对话上下文可能不足以在不同LLM间保持回复一致性，强调需要建立基础设施和设计策略以应对LLM快速持续演进带来的响应稳定性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在不同大语言模型之间，生成回复的语义一致性是否能够维持，尤其是在实际对话场景中，以评估当前提示和上下文对响应一致性的有效性。

Method: 基于真实协作对话数据，比较不同大语言模型在有和无前序聊天历史条件下生成回复的语义相似性，分析模型选择和对话上下文的影响。

Result: 模型选择和对话上下文均显著影响生成回复的语义相似性及与人类回复的一致性；仅靠提示和上下文无法确保跨模型响应的一致性。

Conclusion: 为应对大语言模型快速演进带来的响应不一致问题，需开发更稳定的基础设施和设计策略，以保障生成回复的可比性和一致性。

Abstract: This study examines whether LLM-generated replies remain semantically consistent when the underlying LLM changes. Using messages from real collaborative conversations, we compared the semantic similarity of generated replies across LLMs under two conditions: with and without preceding chat history. Results show that model choice and conversational context both affect response similarity and alignment with human replies. These findings indicate that prompting and conversational context alone may not be sufficient to preserve response consistency across LLMs, highlighting the need for infrastructure and design strategies that can maintain stable and comparable responses amid the rapid and continuous evolution of LLMs.

</details>


### [93] [The Dialect Tax: Dialectal Biases Persist throughout the Language Modeling Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24952)
*Elle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了语言模型在不同方言间的表现差距，并发现这种差距贯穿自然语言处理全流程。即使语义相同，方言文本在分词、预训练、后训练和推理阶段仍存在表征差异。使用字符级反事实分词器也无法消除这些不平等。预训练中，方言对的梯度更新更分散，表明模型学习等义方言内容比学习无关标准英语文本更困难。后训练阶段，奖励模型对孤立的非裔美国英语（AAVE）词汇偏好更高，但在完整推理上下文中则出现任务和模型依赖的方言惩罚。总体而言，方言税并非由单一环节造成，而是累积于整个建模过程之中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在不同方言间的性能差距已被广泛记录，但其根源尚不明确。本文旨在追溯这一‘方言税’在现代语言建模流程中的具体表现与积累机制。

Method: 采用保持语义一致但表面形式不同的平行英语方言语料库，系统评估语言模型在分词、预训练、后训练及推理阶段对标准美式英语（SAE）与方言文本的处理差异。通过对比分析，引入字符级反事实分词器进行消融实验，考察各阶段的表征不对称性与性能差距。

Result: 语言模型在所有阶段均表现出对方言文本的不平等编码：分词阶段存在输入输出不对称；预训练中方言对引发更剧烈的梯度差异；后训练阶段奖励模型呈现不稳定且上下文依赖的方言偏好或惩罚；即使采用字符级分词，问题依然存在。

Conclusion: 方言税并非源于某个单一环节，而是在语言建模的每一个步骤中被编码并逐步累积，表明需从全流程角度重新审视模型的公平性设计。

Abstract: Systematic dialectal performance gaps in language models (LMs) are well documented, but the source of these disparities within the modern language modeling pipeline remains unclear. Our study traces this "dialect tax" across the natural language processing pipeline. Using parallel English dialect corpora that hold meaning fixed while varying surface form, we first confirm that LMs recognize matched Standard American English (SAE) and dialectal texts as semantically equivalent. However, we discover further representational gaps corresponding to downstream performance gaps. Across model families and generations, modern LMs still encode dialectal texts unequally during tokenization, pre-training, post-training, and inference. Strikingly, bypassing traditional subword segmentation via a character-level counterfactual tokenizer removes neither input and output asymmetries nor dialectal accuracy gaps. During pre-training, dialect pairs induce more divergent gradient updates than pairs of entirely unrelated SAE documents, indicating that models find semantically equivalent dialectal content harder to learn from than unrelated SAE documents. During post-training, reward models show contextual, unstable dialect preferences, assigning higher values to isolated AAVE-exclusive tokens than to SAE-exclusive tokens, while full reasoning contexts receive task- and model-dependent dialect penalties. Overall, our findings suggest that the dialect tax is encoded and accumulated not by any one step in isolation, but at every step of the language modeling process.

</details>


### [94] [Unsupervised Post-Training of Foundation Models: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24982)
*Yijie Xu,Qianyi Cai,Huizai Yao,Yili Wang,Tianfu Wang,Cehao Yang,Xingbo Yao,Zhiyu Guo,Aiwei Liu,Xuming Hu,Weiyu Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究无监督后训练（UPT），即在无标签输入上通过模型自身生成的学习信号进行更新，而非依赖外部标注或强教师模型。作者系统整理了80种严格的UPT方法，并按更新信号来源分为四类：预测统计量、样本关系、自生成目标和内部评估器。文章进一步揭示信号选择与任务结构如何影响模型性能，可能提升能力或放大错误。提出输入可见性与更新持久性二维框架，统一指导UPT的选择与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型后训练依赖人工标注、偏好数据、强教师模型或可执行验证器，成本高且难以扩展。为降低对标注的依赖，亟需探索基于模型自身生成信号的无监督后训练方法。

Method: 系统梳理80种严格定义的无监督后训练方法，按更新信号来源分类；构建输入可见性 × 更新持久性分析框架，用于映射部署场景并指导方法选择与评估。

Result: 揭示了不同内部信号与任务结构对模型性能的影响机制，证明部分UPT可有效提升模型表现，而另一些可能导致误差递归放大；提出的二维框架为UPT方法的筛选与评估提供了统一标准。

Conclusion: 无监督后训练可通过模型自身生成的信号实现有效适应，但其效果高度依赖信号设计与任务结构。输入可见性与更新持久性构成关键决策维度，为未来UPT方法的设计与应用提供理论支持与实践指导。

Abstract: Foundation-model post-training usually relies on human labels, preference data, stronger teachers, or executable verifiers. We study Unsupervised Post-Training (UPT): update-bearing adaptation on unlabeled inputs whose learning signal is derived from same-lineage model artifacts rather than an external oracle. We catalog 80 strict UPT methods and organize them by the object that supplies the update signal: a prediction statistic, a sample relation, a self-generated target, or an internal evaluator. Beyond inventory, we show how the choice of internal signal and task structure determines whether post-training improves the model or recursively amplifies error. An orthogonal Input Visibility $\times$ Update Persistence view maps deployment regimes and defines a unified framework for UPT selection and evaluation.

</details>


### [95] [Does Fine-Tuning Undo Activation Steering? Behavioural Recovery Without Weight-Edit Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24988)
*Philipp E. Glass,Allan Tucker,Yongmin Li,Alina Miron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 嵌入式激活引导在语言模型权重中直接编码，可在推理时无需干预的情况下塑造行为，但模型部署后常进行微调，其有效性尚不明确。本研究考察了五种指令微调模型（3B-14B）在非对抗性SFT和RLHF下拒绝抑制与简洁性诱导的嵌入式引导稳定性。行为层面，引导效果随训练数据变化：当优化压力与目标行为冲突时，引导效果下降；反之则保持稳定，其中拒绝消融在SFT下平均损失64%效果。机制层面，权重修改几乎未被改变：均向量恢复率ρ=0.004，微调更新方向与原始引导权重模式近乎正交（平均cosθ=0.074）。即使行为退化，微调也未通过破坏或逆转引导机制实现。因此，嵌入式引导在机制上具有持久性，但在功能上易受干扰，需在下游训练后重新验证行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究嵌入式激活引导在模型部署后微调过程中的稳定性，以评估其在实际应用中的可靠性，尤其关注其是否能在持续训练中保持预期行为。

Method: 对五种指令微调语言模型（3B-14B）在非对抗性SFT和RLHF条件下，测试拒绝抑制与简洁性诱导的嵌入式引导效果，通过行为分析与机制追踪（如权重向量恢复率、更新方向正交性）评估其稳定性。

Result: 行为上，引导效果随训练数据和优化压力变化，当冲突时显著下降（如拒绝消融平均损失64%）；机制上，权重编辑几乎未被改变（ρ=0.004，cosθ=0.074），表明机制持久但功能脆弱。

Conclusion: 嵌入式引导在机制上具有高度耐久性，但在功能上易受下游训练影响，建议在微调后重新验证其行为效果。

Abstract: Activation steering can be embedded directly into a language model's weights, shaping behaviour without inference-time intervention and offering a way to encode alignment prior to release. However, models are routinely fine-tuned after deployment, and it is unknown whether embedded interventions survive this. We study the stability of embedded steering for refusal suppression and brevity induction across five instruction-tuned models (3B-14B) under non-adversarial SFT and RLHF. Behaviourally, preservation tracks the training data: steering degrades when optimisation pressure contradicts the targeted behaviour and persists otherwise, with refusal ablation losing 64% of its effect on average under SFT. Mechanistically, however, the weight edit survives almost untouched even where behaviour reverts: mean vector recovery is $ρ= 0.004$, and the fine-tuning update along the steering direction is near-orthogonal to its pre-edit weight pattern (mean $\cosθ= 0.074$). When steered behaviour degrades, fine-tuning does not achieve it by dismantling or reversing the steering mechanism itself. Embedded steering is therefore mechanistically durable but functionally vulnerable, and requires behavioural re-validation after downstream training.

</details>


### [96] [The Imperfective Paradox Is Not Necessarily in Large Language Models: A Benchmark Failure Before a Model Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25005)
*Kaiqiao Han,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究重新审视了关于不完美进行时悖论的自然语言推理（NLI）基准，指出其受概念和评估设定错误的严重影响。发现语义简化、事件语义标准不一致等问题导致原结论不可靠。通过构建词汇匹配的最小对比对并采用多步推理框架评估，揭示模型存在‘充分性偏差’，即虽未确认完成状态却接受简单过去时假设。此外，提示干预引发标签决策偏移但未提升深层理解。中间步骤与最优引导分析还识别出组合性时态分类错误和表面形式吸引等新失败模式。实验表明，某些大模型在适当提示下表现出与人类相当的语义敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验现有基于不完美进行时悖论的NLI基准的有效性，探究模型为何频繁从进行时描述推断完成事件，并评估提示干预的实际效果。
